## Fork sweep + GPU image/shader pipeline research (2026-11-22, read-only)
- User asked to re-check dipa-studio/ios-sweet forks for working Mali GPU support and how they build the IPSW/img with shaders. Two read-only research agents; full build-pipeline report at `/tmp/opencode/gpu-build-research/FINAL_REPORT.md`. No code, CI, device, boot or partition changes.
- **Fork sweep:** upstream reports 1053 forks; GitHub API returned 536 fork repos (78 pushed since 2026-11-15, 27 created since then). Repo searches for recent S21/Exynos2100/Mali-G78 XNU returned 0. **No working S21/Exynos 2100/Mali-G78 ios-sweet fork found** (again). API-verified fork parents: `Nik300/ios-viva`, `maulanasdqn/skyios`, `aTechGeekDev/ios-apollo`, `99rocker/ios-o1s`, `ahenyao/ios-sweet` are direct ios-sweet forks; `Nik300/viva-gpu`, `stormtroopxyz/y2sios`, PixOS, Jesvi are independent.
- **Only ios-sweet fork with credible full Mali UI:** `Nik300/ios-viva` branch `viva` (pushed 2026-11-19) + `Nik300/viva-gpu`: Helio G96 Mali-G57 MC2, full Metal/CoreAnimation/SpringBoard/Safari; `uapi/README.md` publishes `IOAcceleratorES` with `VivaMTLDriver`/`VMDevice` (UAPI 1.2); QuartzCore 141/141 shaders on the direct Valhall ISA path (`VMG_ISA=1`, `driver/isa/gen_va_v9.py`, Mesa patch `0006-panfrost-precompiled-shader.patch`). Not G78 and not Exynos; retargeting is a guess.
- Other working ios-sweet forks are **Adreno**, not Mali: `skyios` (Adreno 618, ~60 fps) and `ios-apollo` (Adreno 650, 25-55 fps). S21/Exynos dead ends rechecked: `99rocker/ios-o1s` README says GPU not supported; `lightuirom/ios-s21` CPU-only; `ahenyao/ios-sweet` (S22+ Exynos 2200) has no GPU and its 0145 patches are Xclipse/RDNA2; `hojun128/s21xnu` README says needed display/GPU drivers do not exist; our tree is still progress-bar-only on device.
- Best references by GPU: exact G78 = PixOS (Pixel 6a, `GPU_ID 0x92020010`, README claims lock screen/Settings drawn at ~50 fps but home icons black, blur missing, and its `sweetgl-eagl` shim was never loaded on device per its own README). Strongest Samsung/Exynos = `stormtroopxyz/y2sios` (S20+ G77, full Metal UI claim) and `jesvi-eulah/Jesvi-iOS` (S20 G77, CoreAnimation via GLES/Panfrost, ~75 fps draw claim, release v0.1.2 on 2026-11-21); both independent projects, not forks. All working claims are maintainer/README evidence, not this session's boot verification.
- **Image build patterns (verified in source):** case-sensitive journaled HFS+ in the existing userdata partition everywhere relevant. Viva: `sudo tools/viva-mkiosroot-macos.sh IPSW OUT.img [16g]` + `tools/viva-img2simg.py IN.img OUT.img`; includes cryptexes/keybag/sweet-root-tweaks. PixOS: `tools/mkiosroot.sh OS.dmg SYSTEMOS.dmg APPOS.dmg OUT.img [SIZE]` and `tools/overlay-ios-root.sh BASE.img OUT.img MALI_TEST GL_TEST [SWEETGL_DYLIB]` (installs `libsweetgl.dylib`, `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES`; dyld policy 0x5a blocks injection, fallback is `LC_LOAD_DYLIB` + re-sign). Jesvi: `sysimage/fetch.sh` (iOS 16.5 20F66 iPhone X DMGs) -> `select-system.py` -> `tools/apfs/mkapfs.py -z -o system.apfs System=sysroot,manifest=obj/system-files.manifest`; **its SKIP_EXT drops `.metallib` files**, which matters for any Metal path.
- **Shader paths:** (a) runtime AIR -> NIR -> Panfrost on G77/G78 (Jesvi `libredwoodair.dylib` reading `-[_MTLFunction bitcodeData]`, z3s AIR notes, Viva AIR->NIR or direct Valhall ISA, PixOS G78 Mesa `PAN_PROD_ID(9,2,2)` row with G57-borrowed tilebuffer values); (b) static Apple AIR -> NIR -> Valhall `SMSP` pack in our hojun lineage: `tools/air/{metallib.py,air2nir.py,airc.c}` with `GPU_ID 0x92020010`, Apple-derived `air_gen.inc` gitignored, consumed by `metal/SweetMetal.m:842` as `/System/Library/Extensions/SweetMetal.bundle/shaders.smsp`. HoJun's `tools/shadergen/shadergen.c` + `.github/workflows/shadergen.yml` generate small Mesa-compiled kernel blit shaders. `metallib.py list/dump FILE OUTDIR [N]` decodes MTLB bitcode; missing `default.metallib` causes CoreAnimation/RenderBox aborts (concrete extraction commands are in the full report).
- Reusable for S21: HFS+ root assembly, keeping framework `.metallib` files in the image, G78 product-ID/Mesa row, AIR->NIR Samsung fixes (16-bit varying widening, varying-by-name linking, UBO slot 0, tiler heap limit), swap-ticket/display concepts. Not portable: boot shim, UFS layout, DECON/display, GPU clocks/regulators/ACPM, dyld policy, kernel UAPI names. Next decision needed from user: whether to pursue G78 Mesa/Panfrost + runtime AIR translation (PixOS/z3s model) or keep the static SMSP path, and whether any build/boot experimentation is wanted (none done here).

## GPU-path comparison with working Mali ports (2026-11-21, source-level; no device action)
- Working stacks: **Jesvi** (Exynos 990/Mali-G77) deliberately keeps backboardd/SpringBoard on **GLES/Panfrost** and hides Metal discovery from legacy processes (`s20/Jesvi-iOS-main/docs/devices/x1slte/display.md:70-73`, `userland/src/inject.c:565-600`); its visible Apple logo is loader paint, and UI presentation uses a full-screen DECON/IDMA IOSurface with AIR compiled at pipeline creation. **viva-gpu** (Mali-G57 Valhall) is a complete Metal stack: `IOAcceleratorES` -> `VivaMTLDriver.bundle/VMDevice`, Apple3 feature profile, actual AIR -> NIR/Panfrost or direct Valhall ISA with NIR fallback, IOSurface import with 64-byte pitch, per-surface fences/promises and swap-wait (`Nik300/viva-gpu@9ebe5e46`, `Nik300/ios-viva@0a66ed3`). A32/Pixel7pro adopt CA/EAGL surfaces as Mali BOs with syncobjs and display flips.
- **Ours:** `xnu/patches/0112-s21-mali-iokit.sh:493-505` publishes `IOAcceleratorES` + `SweetMetal`; `sweetmetal=2` lets backboardd get a device; 481 name-keyed `SMSP` shaders; `sweet_mali_blit()` compute-copies one layer into the fixed FB at `0xfa000000` (`0112:1143-1392`, `0121`). CPU mode uses `AppleCLCD::swap()` memcpy of the same layer surfaces (`0046`). Feature profile already claims Apple1-3/Common1 and no imageblocks (`metal/SweetMetal.m:4514-4522`), so profile selection is not the obvious gap.
- **Inference:** CPU mode proves panel, layer selection and copy work. Giving backboardd SweetMetal switches CoreAnimation to our incomplete renderer; `gpucomp ok` only proves a copy ran, not that the source layer has pixels. Ranked suspects: (1) CA source layer black/stale, (2) cache/coherency (`sm_target_noflush`, selective source flush, cached BOs), (3) fence/ticket/surface-tag mismatch, (4) shader/variant semantics despite name coverage. Progress-bar-only is consistent with a simple early layer surviving while complex CA draws produce nothing.
- **Cheap discriminators:** boot the 0208 probe kernel (the script now refuses kernels without `fb src %u layer`) and capture `fb src ... nz A/16`; run `SWEETMETAL=1 GPUCOMP=1` (CPU render + GPU blit) and `SWEETMETAL=2 GPUCOMP=0` (Metal render + CPU copy). `source nz=0` => renderer/content; `source nz>0` + black destination => blit/coherency; both nonzero but black panel => present timing. The script has no `EXTRA` boot-arg passthrough, so flushing knobs need a manual cmdline edit or a small script change.
- **Strategic options:** (a) Jesvi-style policy: hide Metal from backboardd, drive UI with Mesa GL/Panfrost (fastest route to visible GPU UI); (b) viva-style full Metal/Panfrost UAPI port (much larger). G57/G77/G710 references are not drop-in for G78 power/MMU/DECON. No code/device changes by this research.

## Fork survey: S21 / Exynos 2100 / Mali-G78 with working GPU (2026-11-21, read-only web/API research)
- **Bottom line (observation):** no fork of `dipa-studio/ios-sweet` with a working Mali-G78 GPU on the Galaxy S21 was found. GitHub API listed **531 forks**; README/NOTES of **527** were scanned (`/tmp/opencode/fork-research/forks.json`, `bulk-report.txt`), plus repo/code search, branch listings and upstream issue #10. Search engines cite ~1354 forks, but the API's actual fork list was used for this survey. No build, boot, flash or device action.
- S21/Exynos 2100 forks found: `99rocker/ios-o1s` (S21 5G SM-G991B, GUI/touch work; README line 32 says "GPU: Not supported"); `lightuirom/ios-s21` (README is unchanged upstream text, CPU rendering, no GPU work); `ahenyao/ios-sweet` (S22+ Exynos 2200, README "No GPU"; its `0145-gpu-display-accel.patch` is IOSurface/commpage plumbing, not a GPU driver); `jimmycarles9991-glitch/s21xnu` and upstream `hojun128/s21xnu` (our S21+ tree: Mali/SweetMetal plumbing and a 481-shader pack, but last reported boots are progress-bar-only). Upstream issue #10 "GPU support" has one comment pointing at Asahi Linux, no implementation.
- **Closest working Mali GPU stacks, none S21/G78 and none ios-sweet forks:** `jesvi-eulah/Jesvi-iOS` (Exynos 990 / Mali-G77; README claims "Accelerated, Metal on the Mali-G77, OpenGL ES through Mesa" — the AIR→NIR→Panfrost stack already audited locally); `Nik300/viva-gpu` (Mali-G57 **Valhall**, full Metal UI at 60/120 Hz, Mesa 26.2.6 Panfrost, AIR→NIR plus direct Valhall ISA); `Kaydn913/hackios-pixel7pro` (Mali-G710, CoreAnimation on Panfrost); `Luminous418/hakios-a32` (Mali-G52, Panfrost + `y2sios` Metal runtime); `steadyakh/HakiOS-Galaxy-S20` (Mali-G77 Panfrost, GPU drawing). `UniXNU/UniXNU/docs/devices/bluejay.md` (Pixel 6a / GS101 **Mali-G78**) explicitly says no Mali component exists there and GPU is not in the first boot milestone; `PixOS-Project/pixelios-kernel` has G78 XNU GPU research docs only.
- **Guess, not proven:** `viva-gpu`'s Valhall AIR→ISA path is the most plausible source-level reference for G78, but retargeting G57→G78 and wiring it into our SweetMetal/blit path is unverified. No code was changed by this survey; raw results are in `/tmp/opencode/fork-research/`, scripts `/tmp/opencode/fork_research*.py`.

## s20/Jesvi iOS audit (2026-11-21, read-only; no device action)
- User asked about booting `s20/Jesvi-iOS_0.1.2_20261121_x1slte.zip` on the S21+ and whether it contains usable compiled shaders. **Observation:** this release is not for our phone. `installer.json`/`candidate.json` target `x1slte` = Galaxy S20 LTE **SM-G980F, Exynos 990**, bootloader `G980FXXSNHYB7`, products `x1s`/`x1slte`; our device is S21+ **t2s / Exynos 2100**. ZIP SHA-256 `7efd523366c328810b80e91df6d6d52762a89bbc5ace9621d30972f005991213` matches its `.sha256` and `build.json`. Source ZIP `Jesvi-iOS-main.zip` SHA-256 `d58f2d6b2f8ea137813a1962883d72ca0ce5d21fd2c44950ac969082edbb9f1d`, source commit `bc4346260887c0b929e79a69303782cd86bc52e3` (clean). `build.json` says `hardware_qualified: false`, `writes_enabled: true`, `native_apfs_checks: true`; `installer_validated: false`.
- This is a **TWRP full-device installer**, not a temporary boot: README requires TWRP 3.7.1+, physical Volume Up holds, and writes `boot.img` (2,445,312 B) to `boot`, `recovery.img` (69,009,408 B) to `recovery`, `system.apfs` (2,658,873,344 B) to `super` offset 0, creates a 64 GB iOS data volume, replaces TWRP, and requires Odin/TWRP to go back. No partition-table changes and no automatic reboot, but it obviously destroys the current Android/TWRP install. `boot.img` is `ANDROID!` bootimg containing XNULOADER v3 and strings `samsung,exynos990` / `x1slte`. Do not flash it on the S21+; AGENTS requires temporary boot images, preserved Android/backups, and explicit confirmation for destructive steps. Extracting `boot.img` for `fastboot boot` on Exynos 2100 is also not viable (different DT/SoC/GPU/display/UFS), guess but strongly indicated by the x1slte/exynos990 profile.
- APFS image was read **without mounting** using the release's own `tools/apfs/apfs.py` (temporary copy at `/home/tom/s21xnu-main/.tmp-jesvi/system.apfs`; unpacked sources at `/tmp/opencode/jesvi-src/Jesvi-iOS-main`). Container: block size 4096, 649,139 blocks (2.66 GB), one volume `System`; 50,810 files / 17,138 dirs / 1,835 symlinks. 117 `.metallib` files. The five corpus libraries total 456 functions: `AppleMetalGLRenderer/default.metallib` 274, `PortraitFilters.cifilter/default.metallib` 36, `portrait_filters.metallib` 135, `AV1SW.metallib` 11, plus QuartzCore/CoreImage/etc. `QuartzCore.framework/default.metallib` contains `variable_blur_frag_lph`, `variable_blur_vert_lph`, `fixed_frag_lph_cph`, etc. Separately, our S21 boot chain has **no APFS support** (`grep -RInE 'apfs' boot*.sh shim xnu/patches` returned no matches); it boots an HFS+ image through `rd=ufsroot0 ufsroot=userdata`/`ufsmap`, so using `system.apfs` would need kernel/loader work even if the device matched.
- **Shader verdict:** these are Apple `MTLB` v1.2 containers of AIR/LLVM bitcode, *not* ready-to-run Mali G78 shader binaries. Jesvi's runtime compiler is `prebuilt/userland/libredwoodair.dylib` (9,879,168 B), built from `tools/mesa/air/` with `air-panfrost.patch` (Mali-G77) and `air-ir3.patch`; it translates AIR → NIR at pipeline creation. Our `SweetMetal.bundle/shaders.smsp` is a different format (`SMSP`, 112-byte headers) containing precompiled Valhall machine code from `tools/air/air2nir.py` + `airc.c`; Jesvi `.metallib` files cannot be copied into it. Local stage packs in `/home/tom/Downloads/sweetbundle-stage` have 480 shaders and include `variable_blur_vert_lph` but not the frag variant; NOTES already records the deployed canonical pack as 481 shaders including the rebuilt `variable_blur_frag_lph`, so the Jesvi image does **not** obviously supply a missing compiled shader. Possible research value is source-level comparison of their `tools/mesa/air/{air_to_nir.c,air_metal.c,air_metallib.c}` and the `libredwoodair` ABI contract against our `air2nir.py`/`airc.c`/`SweetMetal.m` semantics (textures, UBOs, varyings) — not proven to fix the current black-screen/present failure.
- User reported A/B `sweetgpucomp=0/1` "does nothing". **Observation:** `boot-hojun-gpu.sh:60` hardcoded `sweetgpucomp=1` and had no `EXTRA` passthrough, so setting `0` in the environment could not change the booted cmdline; the script's printed `CMDLINE:` is authoritative. Edited `boot-hojun-gpu.sh` to add `GPUCOMP=${GPUCOMP:-1}` and use it in both the cmdline and the echo; default behaviour is unchanged. `bash -n` passes. If a future A/B with confirmed `CMDLINE:` still looks identical, the blit is ruled out and both CPU/GPU copies are seeing the same empty source, moving the bug upstream to SweetMetal/IOSurface content.
- No device, boot, flash, partition, kexec, or SSH operation was performed. Next decision needed from user: (a) read-only translator/ABI comparison for shader correctness, (b) regenerate our own pack from our iOS 16.7 `.metallib` files, or (c) leave this alone and keep debugging the GPU present/compositor path.

## gpucomp=0 boot log + SweetMetal device gate (2026-11-21, read-only)
- User booted with `gpucomp=0` and reports iOS comes up but app open / app-switch swipe is "a lil slow". Log `/home/tom/Downloads/phone-log-20261121-101737.txt` (4,282 lines). **Observation:** `SweetMTL: refused device for backboardd` (line 200), `... for SpringBoard` (line 1540), then chronod/FitnessWidget/MapKit too; no `SweetMTL: shader pack:` line anywhere. Presents are CPU-only: `fb present 1..5 layers gpu 0 cpu 1 mask 1` (lines 1042-1097), only 6 `fb present` / 16 `SWEET FB: swap`, no `alive`/`gpucomp` lines. SpringBoard woke the CPU 45,015 times in ~8 s (line 1069) and `dvfs cputop` shows backboardd+SpringBoard dominating.
- **Cause (source-verified):** `metal/SweetMetal.m:sm_process_allowed()` parses `kern.bootargs`; `sweetmetal=1` allows only `malitest`, `sweetmetal=2` adds `backboardd`/`AegirPoster`/`PosterBoard`, `sweetmetal=4` allows every process. `boot-hojun-gpu.sh` still hard-coded `sweetmetal=1`, so neither backboardd nor SpringBoard ever got a Metal device. This is a different failure from the blit path: `sweetgpucomp=0` only chooses a CPU copy for the final present; it cannot create GPU-rendered content. The slowness is consistent with software compositing (observation; not yet proven by a corrected boot).
- **Fix applied:** `boot-hojun-gpu.sh` now has `SWEETMETAL=${SWEETMETAL:-4}` alongside `GPUCOMP=${GPUCOMP:-1}`, used in both `CMDLINE:` and the GPU-args echo. Default `4` matches the 2026-11-17 interactive/METAL=4 milestone; `SWEETMETAL=2` is available if other clients crash on untranslated shaders. `bash -n` passes. No boot/kexec/device action.
- **Next boot (user must run):** `SWEETMETAL=4 GPUCOMP=1 ./boot-hojun-gpu.sh` with `DO_KEXEC=1` only if the user explicitly asks for kexec. Confirm printed `CMDLINE:` contains `sweetmetal=4 sweetgpucomp=1`. In the new log look for `SweetMTL: +load in backboardd`, `SweetMTL: shader pack:`, `fb present ... gpu 1 cpu 0`, `SWEET FB: swap` cadence, and absence of `refused device for backboardd`. If still slow/black, retry `SWEETMETAL=2 GPUCOMP=1` and compare; then use a source-pixel probe / full-framebuffer paint test to separate render content from the present/copy path.

## METAL=4 GPUCOMP=1 boot still bar-only (2026-11-21, read-only log analysis)
- User ran `SWEETMETAL=4 GPUCOMP=1` and reports "no apple or render, only shows progress bar". Log `/home/tom/Downloads/phone-log-20261121-102549.txt` (3,029 lines, timestamps 505.5-561.6 s). **Device gate fix worked:** `SweetMTL: +load in backboardd` (line 238), `shader pack: 481 shaders` (256), `variable_blur_frag_lph` translated and pipeline `ok` (391-395), no `refused device`, no `texture bo alloc failed`. So the earlier slow boot was indeed CPU-only from `sweetmetal=1`, but granting Metal does not by itself produce visible content.
- **Present path:** first 5 presents are `gpu 1 cpu 0` at 511.95-515.91 s, but their destination probes are black: `mid 00000000 q 00000000` in all 6 logged presents (patch 0122 samples fPixels at center and quarter). Per the earlier 0122 caveat (NOTES line ~178), these are pixel samples, not fences — a dark/uniform framebuffer can coexist with a successful copy — so this supports but does not prove a blit failure. `gpucomp: ok=1 fail=0` (1266), avg flush 492 us / job 11547 us. `alive 30s commits=6 bo 46MB/19` at 537.15 s. At 554.78 s `fb present 6 layers gpu 0 cpu 0` (no layer copied), immediately followed by `SWEET FB: display off`; display on again at 558.01 s, `SWEET FB: swap 10 ... surface 3` at 558.59 s, then log ends at 561.6 s **before any further present**. Only 6 swaps/presents total (vs 16 swaps in the 2026-11-20 METAL=2 log); the log covers only ~55 s after backboardd load, ending just as lock-screen widgets start, so it is too short to rule out later UI.
- **METAL=4 noise/regression:** 66 `NOT in pack` and 13 `fallback` lines, all from SpringBoard compute pipelines (`D8F18H/V`, `D2F10H/V`, `F9H/V`, `U16P3F3H/V`) and chronod `plane__vertex`/`plane_color_fragment` (`pipeline vs=missing fs=fallback` at 2497). The 2026-11-20 METAL=2 log with the same 481-shader pack had 0 of each, so 4 adds untranslated clients without improving the display. Reverted `boot-hojun-gpu.sh` default to `SWEETMETAL=2` (still overridable; `bash -n` passes); keep `GPUCOMP=1`. No boot/kexec/device action by assistant.
- **Large uploads not yet in this log:** only 3 LUT uploads (4096x1 pf25, 1024x1 pf55, 4096x1 pf55, all mostly nonzero) at 506.77 s; no pf80/pf1 image uploads. In the 2026-11-20 log those started ~52 s after backboardd load, i.e. right at this log's end, so their absence is not yet evidence of a decode failure. Also 0 `astc stats`, 0 `skipping draw`, 0 `missing vertex`, no panic.
- **Next:** (1) ask user for a longer pmsg capture from a `SWEETMETAL=2 GPUCOMP=1` boot, at least 3-5 min past backboardd load (kernel timestamp ~700-900 s) so late presents/uploads are visible; (2) if still black, apply/build kernel patch `0208-s21-fb-source-probe.patch` (absent from current kernel `14496999309.zip`: `grep -a` for `fb src %u layer` fails) to log the first 8 source-layer pixels before either copy — this separates "backboardd rendered black" from "blit/coherency wrote black"; patch 0122 remains for the destination probe. `gh` is not installed locally, so a CI kernel rebuild needs the user's runner/credentials. No destructive step approved or performed.

## boot-hojun-gpu.sh now auto-kexecs + auto-password (2026-11-21, local edit only)

## Jesvi source: how it actually gets Apple logo + render (2026-11-21, read-only)
- User was frustrated that the S21 still shows only a progress bar and asked us to read the Jesvi source properly. Extracted `s20/Jesvi-iOS-main.zip` into `/home/tom/s21xnu-main/s20/Jesvi-iOS-main/` (1867 files; read-only audit, no device action).
- **Apple logo is not Metal/GPU at all.** `docs/devices/x1slte/boot-screen.md` + `xnuloader/loader.c:4790-4830` (`boot_logo()`) say the picture is `ProgressUI.framework/apple-logo@3x~iphone.png` taken from the iOS root at build time by `tools/bootlogo/mkbootlogo.py`, RLE-encoded into `xnuloader/applelogo.h`, and drawn as white-on-black pixels into the firmware GOP framebuffer by the loader before iOS starts. The same generator patches Samsung's `up_param/logo.jpg` and the UEFI BootLogo BMP; `MiniIOMobileFramebuffer.cpp:4681,4898` (`bootPictureScale`) scales that loader picture when the panel switches 1440x3200 -> 1080x2400 so the logo stays centered. It is a static pre-kernel framebuffer paint, independent of backboardd/Metal/shaders.
- **iOS UI/progress rendering is a different stack:** `RedwoodMetal.bundle` (`userland/src/redwoodmetal.m`, `redwooddrawable.m`) -> `libredwoodmetal.dylib` + `libredwoodair.dylib` (Mesa `tools/mesa/air/`, AIR -> NIR -> **panfrost** on the Exynos Mali-G77; kernel `RedwoodMali.cpp` user client). `build-gpu-y2s.sh` builds `DRIVERS=softpipe,panfrost AIR=1 tools/mk-mesa.sh`. Presentation is `MiniIOMobileFramebuffer.cpp` with DECON/IDMA, a display pool and `fb_scaler` — not a fixed-size CPU/Mali copy of one layer into `fa000000`. Shaders are translated from Apple `.metallib` at pipeline creation, so there is no precompiled `shaders.smsp` coverage gap like ours.
- **Portability:** none of xnuloader/UEFI/up_param exists on our S21 kexec path. But our shim already owns the same panel framebuffer: `shim/console.h:4` `FB_BASE 0xfa000000`, 1080x2400, stride 4320; kernel `fb present ... fb=fa000000 size=9e3400`. So the *idea* is portable: generate an RLE `applelogo.h` from our own iOS root's ProgressUI PNG and paint it in `shim/loader.c` before `kexec`, then let `sweetfb_noconsole=1` keep it until backboardd presents. That fixes the missing Apple logo only; it does not fix the black UI.
- New log `/home/tom/Downloads/phone-log-20261121-104434.txt` (3,703 lines, SWEETMETAL=2/GPUCOMP=1): 481-shader pack, `+load in backboardd`, `fb present 1..6 ... gpu 1 cpu 0 mid 00000000 q 00000000`, `gpucomp ok=1`, `alive 60s commits=58` and 18 swaps by 507 s. Only six `fb present` lines are expected — patch 0122 logs the first 6 then every 600th — so this is **not** proof presents stopped. SpringBoard/chronod refusals are expected under `sweetmetal=2`. Screen still bar-only with active swaps/commits means the remaining fault is content or the copy/present path, not shader coverage. No boot/device action by assistant.

Hunk 2 was written for line 397 but 0137 added ~280 lines, so the real location is 677; GNU patch found it with fuzz 1, but the whole patch failed because hunk 1 did not match.

## Log 112258 analysis (2026-11-21, read-only; no device action)
- User supplied `/home/tom/Downloads/phone-log-20261121-112258.txt` (3,885 lines, kernel time 457.1-524.6 s) after the `-v` fix. **No verbose boot text at the head of the pmsg capture** (starts at 457 s with userspace sandbox/memorystatus lines), consistent with `VERBOSE=0` taking effect, but the log does not contain the boot cmdline itself so only the printed `CMDLINE:` on the device can confirm.
- Plumbing healthy, same shape as 104434: `backboardd` `SweetMTL: +load` (221), `shader pack: 481 shaders` (250), **0** `NOT in pack`, **0** `fallback`, **0** `skipping draw`, **0** `missing vertex`, **0** panic. `refused device` x4 = SpringBoard/chronod/FitnessWidget under `sweetmetal=2` (expected). `gpucomp: ok=1 fail=0` (1218), 16 `SWEET FB: swap` (all `layer 0`, surfaces 2/3, 1125x2436 fmt 0x62336138), 6 `fb present ... gpu 1 cpu 0` (first `mask 80000007`, rest `mask 1`), all with destination `mid 00000000 q 00000000`. `alive 30s commits=6 bo 45MB/17` (1497), `alive 60s commits=58 bo 94MB/43` (3314). `SWEET FB: display on` 462.68/509.16, off 488.2... actually off at 506.5, on 509.16. Screen still bar-only per user; plumbing is not the blocker.
- Two content-side anomalies in this log:
  1. `texture bo alloc failed 3090x3090 fmt 550` x2 at 518.85 s (3433/3439). 3090x3090 at 4 B/texel ~= 36.4 MiB > the kernel's `SM_MAX_BO_BYTES` 15 MiB cap (`xnu/patches/0112-s21-mali-iokit.sh:55`), so `sm_bo_alloc` returns false and `initWithDevice:descriptor:` returns nil — that texture is never rendered. fmt 550 ~ BGRA8-like. It did **not** appear in the earlier 104434/102549 logs; cause/impact (likely a large app/wallpaper snapshot) not established. Raising the cap is not free: the 15 MiB limit exists because >=16 MiB BOs stalled the system (NOTES 2026-11 history).
  2. `astc stats pf204 1125x2436 (282x609 blocks 4x4): mean rgb 0 0 0, dark 100%` at 518.70 s (3395). This is the CoreAnimation `newTextureWithBytesNoCopy` wallpaper snapshot (`buffer texture nocopy astc 1125x2436 pf204 len 2752512 -> ok`, 3383). Source bytes `fffffffffffffdfc ffff000000000000` are a valid ASTC **void-extent** block whose 8-bit colour fields are all zero, and the decoded store `blk0 ff000000ff000000` is transparent/black. So this texture is genuinely blank in the source CA handed us; decoder handling is correct (void-extent path in `metal/astc_dec.c:1145`). Could be a legitimate clear/blank snapshot, not a proven bug.
- **Conclusion:** no new fatal signal; the remaining fault is still content vs source-copy, which patch 0208 (`fb src %u layer ... nz %u/16`) would separate. 0208 is absent from the kernel the boot script uses (`14496999309.zip` artifact) and did not land in the CI kernel from current main (run 44897382209; `fb src %u layer`/`sweet_fb_srcprobes` strings absent, because main's 145-patch tree lacks the 33-patch Wi-Fi/0201-0207 series that the working 177-patch lineage `9b3e3e9` has). To get a usable probe kernel requires repairing canonical main to the 177-patch lineage and re-pushing (force-push, needs user approval). No boot/kexec/flash/partition action by assistant.

## Verbose console was overwriting the shim logo (2026-11-21, fixed in boot script)
- User's second boot report: "logo still rendered first w green square then text ovewrite it now jst bar." Cause: `boot-hojun-gpu.sh` cmdline still had `-v`, so XNU drew verbose text into the framebuffer console immediately. Patch 0067's `sweetfb_noconsole=1` only sets `sweet_console_muted=1` **at the first swap**, so the boot text scrolled down over the centered shim logo before iOS's boot layer arrived. The logo and green GPU marker themselves rendered correctly.

## Shim now paints the Jesvi-style Apple logo before kexec (2026-11-21, built+staged locally)
- Implemented the portable part of the Jesvi finding. New `tools/bootlogo/mkapplelogo.py` is a self-contained port of Jesvi's `cgbi.py` + `mkbootlogo.py` RLE path: reads `/home/tom/pf_stage/ProgressUI.framework/apple-logo@3x~iphone.png` (CgBI PNG 194x237, alpha coverage min 0/max 255, verified ASCII art is the Apple logo), writes `shim/applelogo.h` with `APPLELOGO_W/H` and 2,962 RLE bytes. - `shim/loader.c` now includes that header with `#if __has_include("applelogo.h")` (build still works without the generated header) and calls `boot_logo()` at the top of `loader_boot()`: `con_clear(0xff000000)` then a centered 194x237 white-on-black paint into `con_fb` (`FB_BASE 0xfa000000`, 1080x2400, stride 4320). Console text afterwards is top-left and `sweetfb_noconsole=1` mutes it on the first swap, so it should not cover the centered logo. - Built with `/home/tom/s21xnu-main/shim/build-s21.sh` (aarch64-linux-gnu-gcc 16.1.0, `bash` clean apart from the pre-existing unused `magic_ok` warning). `out/Image` 249,856 B, SHA-256 `a9f9fef465721d38700cb79035ee6b83b0d7a48e4f8f9a880dafdf30b792358f`; the RLE bytes are present at file offset 17,328 (`applelogo_rle` at 0x43b0). Copied over the staged `/home/tom/ios-sweet/s21-shim.Image` (old `f74cbd1b…` backed up to `/tmp/opencode/s21-shim.Image.pre-logo-20261121`), so the next `./boot-hojun-gpu.sh` will stage the logo build. **Not booted yet** — only the user can kexec.
- `.gitignore` now ignores `shim/applelogo.h` (Apple artwork derived) and the extracted `s20/Jesvi-iOS-main/` tree.
- **Expectation / caveat:** this is the pre-kernel logo only. If the "bar" the user sees is iOS ProgressUI's own full-screen boot layer, its first swap will cover this picture, and the missing Apple logo inside that layer is still a SweetMetal content bug. The next boot/photo will distinguish "logo visible early then bar" from "logo never appears". ## UFS image defrag on device (2026-11-19)
- Assistant SSHed to `root@192.168.1.66` with password `root` via an `SSH_ASKPASS` helper (host has no `sshpass`/`expect`). Device is Arch Linux `7.3.0-rc5 aarch64`; `/mnt/android-data` is F2FS on `/dev/sda34` with 27 GB free at start.
- User ran `boot0105.sh`; it stopped at line 34: `filefrag -v /mnt/android-data/linux/iosroot.img` reported **116,565 extents** / 4,194,304 blocks, above the kernel's 16,384-entry UFS map cap. F2FS has no `f2fs_io` installed and `e4defrag` is ext4-only, so a clean sequential rewrite was used.
- `dd if=iosroot.img of=iosroot.defrag.img bs=4M conv=fsync` (183 s, 93.6 MB/s). New file: **3,975 extents**, SHA-256 `406f2d4a63b1fa58bd7525c8bebfb7eefedebafc206f1ce399902d443a9fbf22`, byte-identical to the original.
- Swapped names; the original fragmented image is preserved as `/mnt/android-data/linux/iosroot.img.frag` (untouched backup). Active `/mnt/android-data/linux/iosroot.img` is the 3,975-extent copy; 11 GB free afterwards.
- Re-ran the exact failing preflight: `filefrag -v ... > /tmp/ff3.txt`, `python3 tools/mkufsmap.py /tmp/ff3.txt ufsmap3.bin`, then the `n <= 16384` assert → **`UFS map entries 3975` PASS**.
- Observation: `/mnt/android-data/linux/rootfs.img` mtime/ctime kept advancing (10:43 → 10:55 UTC) while `fuser` and a `/proc/*/fd` scan found no holder. Not touched by us and not used by `boot0105.sh`; cause unknown (guess: F2FS lazy-time/background metadata, unverified).
- No boot, kexec, flash or partition operations by the assistant. Ready for the user to re-run `~/s21xnu-main/boot0105.sh`.
- User re-ran `boot0105.sh` successfully through the handoff: preflight `extents 3975 blocks 4194304` / `UFS map entries 3975`, `GPU args: mali=1 gpu_scratch=0x93fe00000 (Mesa/Panfrost; no SweetMetal/GPU compositing)`, payload built and scp'd, `jumping` printed, then `kexec -e`. The `Can't open (/proc/kcore)` / VA_BITS warnings come from `kexec` and are benign. Assistant did not initiate the boot.
- User attached `/home/tom/Pictures/IMG_0509.jpg`, but this model cannot view images. Awaiting the user's description of the screen/console before recording the boot result.
- **Boot result (user transcription of console): early panic, before root mount.** Tail includes `vm_page_bootstrap: 0 free pages, -1213486311 wired pages, (up to 0 of which are delayed free)`, then `panic(cpu 0 caller 0xfffffe0007ae4480): Invalid waitq: 0xfffffe0007cff168 @waitq.c:1896`, repeated nested panics from `0xfffffe0007ad5510`, and `In Nested panic stall. Stage 2 bailout`. No Mesa/SweetGL output, no UI.
- Symbol resolution with `llvm-nm-22` on the current kernel: `0xfffffe0007ae4480` = `_waitq_unlock.cold.1`, `0xfffffe0007ad5510` = `_panic_with_options`, `0xfffffe0007cff168` = `_g_boot_waitq`. In this XNU, `waitq_valid()` requires `wq_q->waitq_interlock.lck_valid`; the boot waitq was not initialized (or its memory was corrupted). `waitq_bootstrap()` normally initializes it via `kmem_alloc(kernel_map, ...)`; the bogus `vm_page_bootstrap` wired-page count suggests the VM bootstrap state is bad. This is a guess about cause, not proven.
- The defragged `ufsmap` is 47,700 bytes; patch 0028 accepts any nonzero multiple of 12 up to `sizeof(su_ext)` (196,608) and its `SWEET UFS: ufsmap ...` log would appear later than this panic. Full console ring still needed before blaming the defrag or the removed SweetMetal boot args.
- Log-ring pull attempted directly from the user's Arch SSH session (no fastboot needed): `/dev/sda34` (mounted at `/mnt/android-data`) last 1025×4096-byte blocks → `/tmp/sweetlog.bin`, scp'd to `/tmp/opencode/sweetlog.bin`. `tools/sweet-log.py` reports `no SWEETLOG header (log never flushed, or wrong area)`. The 16 GiB F2FS rewrite likely reused those trailing blocks, so the ring is gone.
- Sanity checks: `xnu/out/kernel.development.sweet` and `kern-new/` still SHA-256 `a50c317aa7c2fd31ca0d7161e8abafb0e73112df65c337e2868119ead7a18442`; `adt.bin` has `dram-base 0x8c0000000`, `dram-size 0x7fd00000` (correct 2 GiB highmem minus carve-outs); payload header `MEM_SIZE 0x7fd00000`.
- QEMU smoke test with the current kernel + current `ufsmap3.bin` in a `--qemu` (lowmem) ADT and the exact boot cmdline ran 30 s under `--fail-on-panic` without reporting a panic; framebuffer text decode came back blank (console mute / QEMU display), so this is not conclusive. Assistant has not initiated another device boot.

## CI failures 44477445342 / 44477533226 and fix (2026-11-19)
- Both runs at head `b60cd4f` completed `failure`. Authenticated logs: XNU `/tmp/opencode/xnu3log/`, userland `/tmp/opencode/userlandlog/`. No device or partition changes.
- **XNU:** the kernel build and artifact collection succeeded; only the post-build step `Verify Wi-Fi stack (0109-0127)` failed with `0127 not applied` (`grep -q s21_plog dxb/xnu/bsd/dev/sweet_pcie.c`). Log line 264 shows `0127-s21-wifi-persistent-log.patch` skipped because `bsd/dev/redwood_bcm4375.c:46` no longer matched. Line 269 shows `0129-s21-pcie-mem-window-kernel-map.patch` skipped as collateral (`bsd/dev/sweet_pcie.c:30`, which expects 0127's `s21_plog` / `swp_logf` context). Other skips are the known set (`0123`, `0124`, `0125`, `0138`, `0139`, `0201`-`0208`, plus older Qualcomm-era/upstream patches).
- **Root cause:** `0142-s21-wifi.sh` runs before all `*.patch` files and refreshes `redwood_bcm4375.c` from pinned z3sios sources. That file now has extra externs and calls `loader_log_line(line)` instead of `printf("%s", line)`. The old 0127 patch was generated against the earlier 0125-generated file, so `git apply --check` failed. This is a patch-context problem, not a missing kernel symbol or compile error.
- **Userland:** the first `for f in userland/*.m` loop linked with only Foundation/Metal/IOKit and failed on `vtprobe.m` before the later duplicate loop (which already had AVFoundation/CoreMedia/VideoToolbox/CoreVideo/QuartzCore) was reached. Undefined symbols were precisely those from the missing frameworks (`AVMediaType*`, `AVPlayer*`, `VTDecompressionSession*`, `CACurrentMediaTime`, `CM*`, `CVPixelBuffer*`).
- Fix commit `041708b` pushed to `origin/main`: regenerated `0127-s21-wifi-persistent-log.patch` against the exact post-`0142` tree, keeping `loader_log_line(line)` and adding `s21_plog(line)`; collapsed the two `.m` loops in `userland.yml` into one link command with all video frameworks.
- Validation: full local patch replay with the fixed 0127 applies it and also makes 0129 apply; the CI source assertions (`s21_plog`, `sweet_wifi_init`, no early `bsd_init` hook, required source files) all pass on the replayed tree. YAML parses. `git -c core.whitespace=-space-before-tab diff --check` is clean (plain `git diff --check` flags space-before-tab in newly added unified-diff context lines from tab-indented z3sios source). No local XNU/macOS compile is possible here.
- Handoff commit `e680585` pushed after `041708b`. Follow-up `d4bdbad` removed the stale `Verify SweetMetal shader pack` step: that assertion was retained during the hojun merge but no step in `userland.yml` generates `shaders.smsp` (the incoming hojun workflow had no such check). The first re-dispatched userland run **44480260973** was allowed to fail at that step so the real build could be checked; it is now complete `failure`. New userland run **44480478277** at `d4bdbad` is queued; XNU run **44480243933** is still in progress at `041708b` (the shader removal does not affect XNU). Handoff commit `519d75e` pushed after `d4bdbad`; remote HEAD verified at `519d75e`.

## CI round 3: 44480243933 / 44480478277 failures and fix (2026-11-19)
- Both runs completed `failure`. Logs: XNU `/tmp/opencode/xnu4log/`, userland `/tmp/opencode/userland2log/`. No device or partition changes.
- XNU: all source assertions passed (0127, 0128, no early hook, required files). Only remaining failure was `kernel lacks string wifissid`. Root cause: `0142-s21-wifi.sh` refreshes z3sios sources at a pinned commit whose S21 glue stubs `redwood_wifi_network()` to return false, so the documented `wifissid=`/`wifipass=` bootarg (and its string) disappeared when the old `0125` patch was skipped. The GPU slice commit `2aca51ea78d7d3a8688f4ed575fa8e3b6ce746e7` was pushed to `origin/main` after user confirmation; remote HEAD verified with `git ls-remote`. Final remote HEAD after the handoff commit: `88d92712e40c3c52c9a7df64c6a5251fa6dcb881`. New files: `userland/patches/mesa-26.2.5/` (PixOS's four MIT Mesa patches, verbatim + attribution README) and `userland/sweetdrm/` (S21 libdrm shim, fake IOKit, host test, README). `.github/workflows/userland.yml` was extended with Mesa 26.2.5 clone/patch, host test, and a codesigned `out/libs21sweetdrm.dylib` iOS arm64 build.
- Why not PixOS verbatim: its `sweet_drm_mali` uses selectors query/boNew/boClose/submit/wait/importSurface with kernel-chosen VAs and seqno returns. This fork's `0112-s21-mali-iokit.sh` uses GET_INFO/CREATE_BO/MAP_BO/SUBMIT(synchronous)/DESTROY_BO/IMPORT_USER with client-chosen VAs and a 15 MiB per-BO cap. The shim splits Mesa's 128 MiB tiler heap into 9 adjacent kernel BOs, synthesizes G78 params from the measured Linux `panparams` dump (§11.21 of `~/s21/S21_Exynos2100.md`), and has no PixOS selector assumptions.
- Exact validation done: `git ls-remote` confirms the `mesa-26.2.5` tag (`f9ce5e81f30cd694726bff6d8197e509a8374ea7`); cloned it to `/tmp/opencode/mesa26-real` and **all four patches apply cleanly in order**. Host test built with `userland/sweetdrm/host-test.sh /tmp/opencode/mesa26-real` and **passed** with clang and with clang `-fsanitize=address,undefined`; also passed with gcc against a patched Mesa 26.2.4 tree in `/tmp/opencode/mesa26-test`. Checks cover GET_PARAM, BO create/map/unmap, submit, 9-way heap splitting, selector-8 import and syncobjs. `git diff --check`, YAML parse and `bash -n` on the script are clean.
- Not validated: the new CI steps have not run (no `gh` locally), no Mesa build/link, no iOS dylib link, no GL clear/readback, no EAGL or AIR/Metal work, no XNU build, no device boot. Next stage after CI is a full Mesa `-Ddarwin-drm=true` iOS build linked with `s21_sweet_drm.c`, then a GL clear/readback test. Already pushed; confirm before any device/partition work.

## GPU comparison research: y2sios, z3sios, G78 forks (2026-11-19, read-only)
- **y2sios `/home/tom/ios-sweet/y2sios-main` and z3sios `/home/tom/ios-sweet/z3sios-main` are Exynos 990 / Mali-G77 MP11 (Valhall) ports, not G78.** Both use the same architecture: CoreAnimation → RedwoodMetal / EAGL+libredwood-gl → libredwoodair (AIR→NIR) or OSMesa → Mesa 25.0.7 panfrost → redwood-drm (libdrm stand-in, `request & 0xff` → IOKit selector) → IOKit `RedwoodMali` (Panfrost UAPI 1.3, selectors 0x40 SUBMIT / 0x41 WAIT_BO / 0x42 CREATE_BO / 0x44 GET_PARAM / 0x7e surface) → `bsd/dev/redwood_mali.c` → G77. Key files: `kernel/xnu/files/{bsd/dev/redwood_mali.c,iokit/Kernel/RedwoodMali.cpp,iokit/Kernel/MiniIOMobileFramebuffer.cpp,iokit/Kernel/MiniIOSurface.cpp}`, `kernel/tools/mesa/{redwood-drm/redwood_drm.c,panfrost-darwin.patch,air/air_metal.c,air/air_to_nir.c}`, `userland/src/{redwoodmetal.m,redwooddrawable.m,eagl.c}`. Display side: loader-reserved contiguous <4 GiB display pool, swap tickets (`fSwapsQueued`/`fSwapsDone`), GPU-done waits before present, Exynos 990 IDMA/DPP channel-5 zero-copy scaler + DECON shadow latch, vsync phased from latch. Proven G77 shader fixes: `widen_16bit_varyings()` (half4 varyings read as 32-bit → black fills), link varyings by AIR name, per-attachment clears, UBO slot 0 as user buffer, process-wide `rwa_*` lock. No G78/Exynos-2100/SweetMetal code in either tree; G77 register values and DECON map must not be copied verbatim.
- **Fork search (GitHub API + web search):** enumerated all 626 forks returned for `dipa-studio/ios-sweet` (API `forks_count` 1024, pages 1–6). The only S21/S22 forks are `99rocker/ios-o1s` (S21 o1s Exynos 2100), `lightuirom/ios-s21` (S21+), `ahenyao/ios-sweet` (S22+ Exynos) — all explicitly say **GPU not supported** / software-only. `hojun128/s21xnu` is a fork of `jimmycarles9991-glitch/s21xnu` (not a GitHub fork of dipa-studio) and uses the hand-written SweetMetal G78 path; local notes record interactive GPU-composited iOS but texture/present regressions. No dipa-studio fork with working Mali-G78 was found.
- **PixOS is the working G78 reference (not a fork; "inspired by ios-sweet"):** `PixOS-Project/pixelios-kernel` README status 2026-11-15 reports GPU acceleration working on Pixel 6a / Tensor G1 at ~50 fps with GPU at 150 MHz (rendered 50.6 fps on runs 155b/8177, panel presents 49.0/s on 18e1). Its GPU is same reported ID as our S21 driver: `GPU_ID 0x92020010` (`0x9202` in Linux notes), `JS_PRESENT=0x7`, JM (not CSF). Structure: shim gates PMU/CMU then enables S2MPG11 BUCK2S rail (root cause of stuck `SHADER_PWRTRANS` was rail, not clock), leaves rail on for XNU, S2MPU read-only; XNU patches 0102/0103/0104/0105/0272 expose `SweetMali` IOKit client (selectors 0 query, 1 boNew, 2 boClose, 3 submit, 4 wait, 5 importSurface, 6 boCacheSync) with explicit cache sync because path is not coherent; fragment→JS0, other jobs→JS1, `JS_CONFIG=0x83B00`, `COHERENCY_ENABLE=31`, 4-level 4K page tables, WRITE_VALUE magic selftest. `docs/research/{17,27,28,30,31,32,33,34,35,36}-*.md` hold measured facts; `wip/gpu-accel` is gone from remote heads (only `main`, `wip/w3a-wlan-link`).
- **PixOS userland:** `PixOS-Project/pixelios-userland/tools/sweetgl-mali/` implements libdrm/Panfrost emulation over the SweetMali user client (`sweet_drm_mali.c`, `request & 0xff` ioctl decode, fake `/dev/null` fd, syncobj/BO table, `importSurface`, `boCacheSync`); `tools/mesa-patches/` adds Mesa 26.2.5 G78 row `PAN_PROD_ID(9,2,2)` (tilebuffer copied from G57 = guess), Darwin fake-libdrm/hook patches 0001–0008; `tools/sweetgl-eagl/` injects `libsweetgl` into backboardd with an EAGL shim (must make its Mesa context current itself). Note stale caveat: `sweetgl-mali/README.md` still says "nothing built/run on iOS" (Oct 23), superseded by the Nov 15 hardware status.
- **Recommendation (not executed):** for S21 prefer Mesa/Panfrost + IOKit UAPI (y2s/z3s templates, PixOS G78 userland/hardware patterns) over extending hand-written SweetMetal; start with G78 model row + libdrm shim + explicit WC/BO cache sync + raw WRITE_VALUE/GL clear tests, then CoreAnimation, then AIR/Metal. Reuse present concepts (swap tickets, GPU-done fences, vsync phase) but re-derive all G3D/PMU/CMU/S2MPU/IRQ/DECON addresses on Exynos 2100; S21 handoff registers at `0x19cb1040/1440/1010` and `0x19f00030` are already verified. No builds, CI runs, boots, or device writes this session; `gh` unavailable.

## Live XNU handoff registers verified (2026-11-18)
- User read the four DPU/DECON registers on the phone immediately before kexec with Python `mmap('/dev/mem')` (Arch BusyBox has no `devmem` applet; `dd` on `/dev/mem` fails `Bad address`):
  - `0x19cb1040 = 0xfa000000` (DPP DMA1 BASE_Y8)
  - `0x19cb1440 = 0xfa000000` (SHD_BASE_Y8)
  - `0x19cb1010 = 0x09600438` (SRC_SIZE: 2400 rows, 1080 words = stride 4320)
  - `0x19f00030 = 0x00003061` (DECON trigger unmasked)
- All four match the expected LK/XNU state. The framebuffer base/stride/scanout handoff is therefore **ruled out** for this tested boot; do not move `0xfa000000` or tune `sweetfb_gate`/`sweetvsync_abs` next. The remaining suspect is content: SweetMetal upload/draw output or `sweet_mali_blit()` source/present path. No register writes or boots were performed by the assistant.
- Staged the next experiment in this snapshot (NOT built/booted, no git repo here so nothing was committed):
  - New `xnu/patches/0208-s21-fb-source-probe.patch` adds `fb src N layer L pa ... off ... bpr ... WxH nz A/16 sum XXXXXXXX` for the first 8 valid 32-bpp layers, sampled from `src + s->offset + sy*bpr + sx*4` before either the GPU blit or CPU copy. It reuses `sweet_mali_log_str()` from 0122 and the existing `fb present ... mid/q` destination probe.
  - `boot0105.sh` now prints the four handoff registers over SSH with Python `mmap('/dev/mem')` before `kexec` (Arch BusyBox lacks `devmem`; `dd` on `/dev/mem` returns `Bad address`).
  - Validation: `git apply --numstat` parses 0208 cleanly (23 insertions); `bash -n boot0105.sh` passes. Full apply-check/compile needs the real xnu tree and CI.
  - Boot plan: one run with `sweetgpucomp=0` and one with `1`, marker off, then read `fb src` plus `fb present` from the pmsg log. Source zero => upload/SweetMetal; source nonzero + GPU dest zero => `sweet_mali_blit()`/coherency; both nonzero but panel black => present timing (only then revisit pacing).

## y2sios GPU/display comparison (2026-11-18, investigation only)
- **Conclusion:** y2sios works because it has both a mature render stack (CoreAnimation → EAGL/libredwood-gl → OSMesa/Mesa 25.0.7 panfrost → redwood-drm → RedwoodMali IOKit → Mali-G77) and a real Exynos 990 DPU present path (contiguous MiniIOSurface pool, IDMA/DPP channel 5 scaler, DECON shadow latch, swap tickets, panel-phase vsync). S21 instead has a custom G78 submit path, hand-written SweetMetal, and a copy into the fixed `fb=0xfa000000` scanned by S-Boot; it does not program an Exynos 2100 DPU swap/scaler path.
- **Green/red square is a kernel diagnostic, not a rendered layer.** `0121-s21-gpu-composite.patch` writes `0xFF00FF00` when every copied layer used `sweet_mali_blit()` and `0xFFFF0000` if any used CPU; `0137` later gates it behind `sweetfbmark=1`. Its visibility proves only that the fixed framebuffer is being scanned.
- `0122`'s `mid`/`q` fields are **destination pixel probes**, not fences. Early presents in `~/Downloads/phone-log-20261118-140430.txt` have `gpu 1 cpu 0 mid 00000000 q 00000000`; `~/Downloads/phone-log-20261118-134056.txt` later has `gpu 0 cpu 1 mid ff1c1c1e q ff1c1c1e`. Thus "copy succeeded" and uniform/dark framebuffer contents can coexist.
- Strongest current evidence points upstream of present: in the 140430 log, 386×386 `pf80` passes report `3 draws`, `clear=1`, job status `fragment=0`, yet `target ... nz=0/16` and `getBytes ... mid 00000000`. Later `pf1`/`pf80` uploads are nonzero, so the failure is selective, not simply "all uploads dead". **Hypothesis, not proven:** SweetMetal's draw/texture binding semantics leave render targets clear despite successful job status.
- The 090701 known-good boot (kernel #74 era, 321712-byte driver) had `fb present` at ~20 Hz and `gpucomp ok=600`, so the simple copy path is capable of showing UI. Do not port Exynos 990 DECON registers to the S21 before bisecting back to that known-good render path.
- Ranked next experiments: (1) marker off and reproduce the known-good recipe; (2) checksum the source layer and both CPU/GPU copies around `sweet_mali_blit()` (`sweetgpucomp=0` vs `1`) to separate source content, blit, and panel timing; (3) replay one zero-output render pass with input texture/descriptor dumps and compare against the 090701 driver; (4) only then A/B `sweetfb_gate=0`/`sweetvsync_abs=0`; (5) longer term port y2s concepts (contiguous surfaces, DPU channel/scaler, shadow latch, swap tickets, TE-aligned vsync) with an Exynos 2100 register map, or adopt Mesa/Panfrost+G78 and the AIR translator. No build or boot was run this session.

## Linux S21+ GPU/framebuffer cross-check (2026-11-18, read-only)
- Linux GPU path is **mainline Panfrost Valhall-JM**, not the vendor DDK: `boot52.log:282-292` shows `mali-g78 id 0x9202`, `shader_present=0xee0cee`, `JS:0x7` (3 job slots), DRM minor 1. DTS node `/home/tom/s21/exynos2100-t2s-firstlight-v32.dts:2442-2454` uses `arm,mali-valhall-jm`, IRQs 339/340/338 and a fake 400 MHz clock; no OPP/power domain/DVFS.
- Mesa 26.2.4 has a G78 model row added; tilebuffer/rates/counters were copied from G57/G77 and are explicitly unverified (`S21_Exynos2100.md:1413-1423`, `mesa-26.2.4/patch_mesa_g78.py`). First jobs faulted with zeroed descriptors until the kernel added `dcache_clean_inval_poc()` for write-combined non-heap BOs at mmap (`S21_Exynos2100.md:1549-1594`, `patch_panfrost_wcflush.py`). With that fix: clear/readback 10/10, no `js fault`; later `cage`/`labwc` Panfrost desktops and Firefox WebGL `Mali-G78 MC14 (Panfrost)` are confirmed (`S21_Exynos2100.md:1697-1739`, `:2766-2787`). Residual ~1% unexplained job faults remain (`:1604-1637`). No GPU firmware blob is loaded.
- **`fb=0xfa000000` is a valid physical base**, observed independently in stock cmdline (`live.dts:856`), bootloader ramoops DPU registers (`dmesg-ramoops-0.txt:30-37`: `DMA1_BASE_Y8`/`SHD_BASE_Y8=fa000000`, `SRC_SIZE=09600438`), DT simplefb (`exynos2100-t2s-firstlight-v32.dts:155-169`), reserved `fb_scanout` pool (`:350-356`, 64 MiB), native scanout boot log (`boot52.log:290-292`), and kexec paint tests (`fbtest.S`, `S21_Exynos2100.md:2246-2252`). XNU's constants in `tools/mkadt.py:31-33`, `shim/console.h:4-7` and `shim/loader.c:136-141` are therefore not arbitrary.
- Open caveats, not reasons to move the base: XNU uses stride **4320** (`1080*4`) while native Linux DRM known-good uses **4352** (`S21_Exynos2100.md:2770-2781`; DPU size word `09600440`). Native Linux flips cycle `fa/fb/fc000000` (`boot64.log:7677-8134`). If XNU is kexec'ed after an active native-DRM GNOME session, DPP DMA1 may point at `fb`/`fc` with 4352 stride; read back `0x19cb1040`, `0x19cb1440`, `0x19cb1010` before kexec. Visible XNU boot bar/green marker means those boots were still scanning `fa000000`.
- Practical conclusion: keep `0xfa000000`; first verify handoff base/stride and source pixel checksums. Linux success validates the hardware and shows the exact cache-coherency class of bug that can make jobs "succeed" while output is zero; it does **not** validate SweetMetal's hand-written shader/texture implementation.

Next: CI build, check for "Skipping patch" lines and link errors, then boot with `sweetpcie=2 sweetwifi=1` and watch `wifi:` / `SWEET WIFI:` console lines.

## S21+ Wi-Fi Stage 2 S2MPU window patch (2026-10-31)
- Added `xnu/patches/0110-sweet-pcie-s2mpu-window.patch`. `sweetpcie=1` retains the confirmed Stage 1 behavior. `sweetpcie=2` opens the Exynos `PCIE_GEN2` DMA pool before assigning BARs/enabling endpoint bus mastering: HVC FID `0xC6000100`, subsystem index 7, RW attr 3, `0x92400000..0x933fffff`, in 512 Linux-compatible 32 KiB calls. It stops and logs the first HVC error.
- XNU's managed low RAM (`0xb0000000` through below `0xfd000000`) and optional high bank (`0x8c0000000+`) do not overlap this pool.
- Patch parser validation (`git apply --numstat`) reports a 47-add/3-remove hunk. No source checkout was available for apply-check/build; no device boot or HVC was run. Next: build, confirm 0110 applied, boot with `sweetpcie=2`, and report the `S2MPU PCIE_GEN2` result before firmware/DMA work.

## S21+ Wi-Fi Stage 3 firmware boot path (2026-10-31)
- Added patch `xnu/patches/0111-sweet-pcie-wifi-firmware.patch`: `sweetpcie=3` performs Stage 1+2, scans the BCM backplane for the ARM CR4/PCIe2/D11 cores, reads the CR4 SRAM bank sizes, halts CR4 and D11, copies firmware at TCM `0x170000`, copies normalized NVRAM at the Linux-derived end-of-RAM address, clears the shared pointer, sets the reset vector and releases CR4, waits up to 5 s for the shared pointer, writes/readbacks host_cap `0x00801407` at offset 84, and logs firmware shared flags. CLM is retained in the reserved payload for the next control/IOCTL stage; it is not yet submitted to firmware.
- `tools/mkpayload.py` now accepts `--wifi-firmware`, `--wifi-nvram`, `--wifi-clm`. It normalizes the text NVRAM like brcmfmac and places the three files in reserved kernel payload segments, passing their physical addresses and lengths as boot arguments. Proprietary blobs remain in `/home/tom/s21/fw`, outside git. `boot0105.sh` supports `WIFI=1 WIFI_NVRAM=/home/tom/s21/fw/nvram.txt_1rh_es43_b1` (or the CS01 file); firmware/CLM paths can be overridden with `WIFI_FW` / `WIFI_CLM`.
- Evidence used: Linux handoff reports BCM4375 chip ID `0x4375`, rev 5, TCM base `0x170000`, BAR2 4 MiB, working firmware banner, and end-to-end Wi-Fi on this phone. The NVRAM choice remains unestablished; script requires the user to select one explicitly.
- Status: Stage 3 patch parses (`git apply --numstat`: 339 additions, 4 removals). No XNU build, local compile, device boot, or firmware download has been performed. Next: build and confirm 0111 was applied, run `WIFI=1 WIFI_NVRAM=... ./boot0105.sh`, then inspect `SWEET PCIE:` logs for CR4/TCM sizing, firmware shared pointer, `host_cap` confirmation, and the RTE banner. Follow up by submitting CLM via the Stage 4 IOCTL path.

## 8-core SMP and touch bring-up (2026-10-31)
**Result (observed by the user):** all 8 cores initialize (4x A55 MPIDR 0x000-0x300, 3x A78 0x400-0x600, 1x X1 0x700) with `cpus=8`; touch works, and swipe-up unlocks to the home screen. UI is much faster than with 1 core. Boot takes noticeably longer than with `cpus=4` (cause not investigated; bring-up starts cores one at a time with per-core waits, which is a guess). Staged boots `CPUS=2`, `CPUS=4`, `CPUS=8` all reached the lock screen/home screen. No long-run stability test has been done yet.

**Fixes (patches `0107`, `0108`, plus `tools/mkadt.py` and `boot0104.sh`):**
- `0107` `pexpert/arm/pe_fiq.c`: the GIC redistributor walk used a fixed `GICR_PE_SIZE` of 0x20000. The S21 DTS gives a 2 MiB window for 8 CPUs, i.e. 0x40000 each (GICv4 frame layout). The walk now steps 0x40000 when `GICR_TYPER.VLPIS` (bit 1) is set, else 0x20000. `tools/mkadt.py` `GICR_SIZE` raised from `0x100000` to `0x200000` (1 MiB only covered 4 CPUs). Not tested in isolation; after this change the failure moved from the redistributor lookup to the `CPU_ON` timeout below.
- `0107` `iokit/Kernel/arm/SweetCPU.cpp`: PSCI `CPU_ON` context id is now 0 (Linux passes 0; earlier probes only worked with 0; the entry stub ignores x0).
- `0108` `SweetCPU.cpp`: with `cpus>1` the kernel panicked `SWEET CPU: cpu=1 startup timeout (reset/MMU/GIC/SGI)` at `SweetCPU.cpp:164`, although `CPU_ON` had been accepted. Cause: the kernel lives at ~0x8c0000000 (above 4 GiB) while every `CPU_ON` that ever worked (Linux, probe payloads) used an entry below 4 GiB. `startCPU` now maps physical `0x80300000` (dead shim image from the kexec) with `ml_io_map`, writes a 16-byte trampoline (`ldr x16,#8; br x16; .quad entry`) there, and passes that address to `CPU_ON`. It worked from the first boot with it (`CPUS=2`). Assumption, not verified: `0x80300000` is unused after the shim hands over. `ml_io_map` must be declared `extern "C"` at file scope (the first 0108 failed to link without it).
- `0094` touch driver (`bsd/dev/sweet_touch.c`, bit-banged I2C on gpp4-0/1, ATTN gpa1-0, polled): `touch_pwr` now defaults to 2 (power-cycle the S2DOS05 LDO2/LDO3 touch supplies) and the device-ID read retries up to 4 times, power-cycling again on each NAK. Touch started working with this build. Likely cause (inferred, not proven): Linux's `slsi_ts` shutdown leaves the controller asleep after kexec. Which part of the change fixed it (power-cycle vs retry) is unknown.
- `boot0104.sh` now uses `cpus=${CPUS:-8}`; run `CPUS=4 ./boot0104.sh` to step down if a core misbehaves.

**Build facts:** CI run with 0107+0108 first failed to link (missing `extern "C"`); the corrected 0108 built. Patches still skipped by CI on the S21 (Qualcomm-era, do not apply on the Exynos `sweet_ufs.c`): 0030, 0031, 0034, 0041, 0047, 0054, 0060, 0062, 0063, 0069, 0072-0077, 0081, 0082, 0089. Check `Skipping patch` lines after every push. Live repo/remote: `github.com/jimmycarles9991-glitch/s21xnu`, branch `main` (remote name `s21`; the older `tiktop101/s21xnu` remote in older notes is stale).

**Open / next:**
- The scheduler treats all 8 cores as identical; nothing steers work to the A78/X1 cores (no AMP/cluster topology in the ADT). Per-core `cluster-id` is 0 for all.
- `CNTFRQ_EL0` is 0 or junk on the secondary cores (firmware never programs it); XNU takes 26 MHz from the ADT so it has not mattered so far.
- No soak test yet: watch for random panics on the big cores and report the first panic line (`cpu=N`).
- Move `0105-sweet-hid-edge-toggle-log.patch` from the repo root into `xnu/patches/` if it should be in the build.
- CPU hotplug/suspend (`CPU_OFF`, resume) is still unimplemented and panics by design.


## Console-mute panic: root cause + fix (2026-10-31)
- IMG_0463 = same panic as IMG_0462 (PC 0xfffffe000745b690 `_switch_to_serial_console+0x1c`, FAR `_cons_ops_index`, LR `_PE_initialize_console+0x58`): booted kernel still has the old `PE_initialize_console(kPEDisableScreen)` path, i.e. **0067 was not applied**. Writing `cons_ops_index` (SECURITY_READ_ONLY_LATE on arm) faults. Boot WITHOUT `sweetfb_noconsole=1` as a stopgap.
- Why 0067 kept being skipped: (1) old hunk headers did not match their bodies (malformed), (2) it anchored on `sweet_log_append` from patch 0047, but on S21 0047 never applies (the S21 `bsd/dev/sweet_ufs.c` from 0028 is Exynos-specific, so Qualcomm-era 0030/0034/0047/0054/0060/0069/0077/0082 fail `git apply --check` and are silently skipped by dxb).
- New 0067 is generated with git/diff against real xnu-8796.141.3 (replayed chain), depends only on pristine `_cnputs` and patch 0046's SweetFramebuffer.cpp; `git apply --check` passes, result byte-identical to the edited tree. Adds `int sweet_console_muted` + early return in `_cnputs` unless `panic_active()`. CI now greps source and `nm`s for `_sweet_console_muted`.
- Next: push, run CI, verify `_sweet_console_muted` (boot0104.sh already guards), boot with `sweetfb_noconsole=1`. Note: the silent-skip list above means the S21 kernel has no persistent log/UFS-fast/touch-driver patches; port those to the Exynos sweet_ufs.c if wanted.


## UFS root journal write failure (2026-10-31)
- User photo shows HFS journal-header writes failing with strategy error `0x1e` (`EROFS`), followed by root mount retries. The ZIP from the first rebuild still lacked all `ufsov` strings, so that boot could not enable writes.
- Root cause confirmed in Actions run `41199990933` logs: patch 0084 was skipped as “corrupt patch at line 26.” The prior edit added a line to a unified diff without adjusting its hunk count. Corrected its header from `+96,19` to `+96,20` in commit `57d2447`, pushed to `jimmycarles9991-glitch/s21xnu:s21-stage2`.
- Corrected Actions run `41202460069` succeeded from `57d2447f`; its log shows patch 0084 applied with no corrupt-patch warning. Extracted its ZIP to `xnu/out` and verified `SHA256SUMS`. Archive SHA-256 `ed88828af019fefd39780fec80d878731d37d4f15f2323866a28efe3ee486daa`; kernel SHA-256 `1f65484e6eab20424a4a1d167af0eeaf854844b482bbac635b96d3b8debefb29`. Kernel strings include overlay setup/failure/success diagnostics and `ufsovmb`. The overlay limit is 2^24 blocks (64 GiB), enough for the 16 GiB extent map. This is a volatile RAM overlay.
- **Boot result from user photos IMG_0460/IMG_0462 (2026-10-31):** IMG_0460 shows launchd, backboardd, and SpringBoard active after the HFS root-mount failures. The repeated `bluetoothd` and `fairplayd.H2` launches are the known nonfatal no-hardware loops (0057 handoff). IMG_0462 shows the later panic caused by `sweetfb_noconsole=1`: PC `0xfffffe000745b690` = `_switch_to_serial_console+0x1c`, LR `0xfffffe0007a76160` = `_PE_initialize_console+0x58`, FAR `0xfffffe00071775e0` = `_cons_ops_index`; the console switch writes to read-only `__DATA_CONST`. Mach-O symbol table has no `_sweet_console_muted`. Actions run `41202460069` logs confirm both 0047 and 0067 were silently skipped; 0067 expected the 0047 `sweet_log_append` context. Reworked 0067 to be independent of 0047 and added a symbol guard to `boot0104.sh`, so it refuses to kexec a kernel without the safe mute path. Need build a kernel with 0067 applied, verify `_sweet_console_muted` exists, then run `boot0104.sh` from the interactive host terminal (agent SSH lacked credentials). No fastboot or partition flash was performed.
- Follow-up run `41207523694` still skipped 0067 at `serial_console.c:281`; its ZIP lacks `_sweet_console_muted` and must not be booted with `sweetfb_noconsole=1`. Re-anchored the patch hunks to XNU 8796's `_cnputs` body (`serial_console.c:250-253`) rather than the displaced line numbers. Patch parses locally; build verification pending.
- Run `41209369541` still skipped 0067 at `serial_console.c:253` because a separate one-line hunk did not match. Folded the mute check and `disableConsoleOutput` declaration into one `_cnputs` hunk with exact function context. Replacement build pending; continue to reject artifacts missing `_sweet_console_muted`.

- **Boot result:** user photo shows `SWEET EARLY PANIC: Synchronous Exception taken while SP1 selected`, PC `0xfffffe0007480b24`, FAR `0x220`, ESR `0x96000006`. Disassembly of the exact artifact identifies the faulting instruction as `ldr x0, [x0, #0x220]` in `_current_percpu_base` (offset +0xc); `x0` was zero. `_current_thread` immediately before that load reads `TPIDR_EL1`, so the active thread pointer was null. The full HFS root did not mount and Hello was not reached. Phone showed XNU panic; userdata image hash remains the verified full-root hash, and no other partition was changed.
- **Panic binary comparison:** the Oct 28 kernel and the macOS 15/Xcode 16 artifact have identical instruction sequences for `_current_percpu_base` and `_machine_set_current_thread`; the latter writes the thread pointer to `TPIDR_EL1`. This does not support an Xcode 16 code-generation regression. The caller and startup ordering that leave `TPIDR_EL1` zero are still unknown. Diagnostic patch `0106-sweet-early-fault-frame.patch` prints the interrupted frame's previous FP and caller LR in `sleh_synchronous_sp1`, before normal panic handling. Run `40952558826` skipped 0106 due to a mismatch with XNU 8796's panic-call syntax; run `40954669761` skipped it because the diff was malformed; run `40955455764` applied the `sleh.c` hunk but skipped the helper hunk because its insertion offset was one line late. None of those artifacts contains the complete trace and none should be booted. Corrected the helper offset in commit `51946b2`; replacement run `40956410872` is pending. After confirming 0106 applied and the frame marker is linked in the artifact, boot only as a temporary kexec image and use the caller LR to inspect startup before changing thread initialization.
- **Arch recovery (2026-10-29):** user says phone is back in Arch. SSH is responsive (`uptime` succeeded; kernel is 7.3.0-rc5 aarch64). Userdata image file still reports 17,179,869,184 bytes / 33,587,440 blocks; `/mnt/android-data` has 3.6 GiB free (97% used). Attempt to read `dmesg` stalled after these quick checks and was interrupted; no new boot log was captured. No image or partition writes performed during recovery check.

**Kernel workflow:** edit `research/bt28/xnu/...` (keep the pre-edit copy in `research/preNNNN/`), syntax check `python3 tools/xnu-cc.py research/run28.log research/bt28 <file>`, make `xnu/patches/NNNN-*.patch` with `diff -u pre bt28 | sed` path rewrite, add new/changed files to `research/check-patch-order.py` list, run it (must say applied + MATCH), commit + push -> GitHub Actions `xnu` builds (~8 min) -> `gh run download <id> -R dipa-studio/ios-sweet -n xnu-kernel -D xnu/out-NNNN`. **Repo is PUBLIC since 2026-10-17** (private macOS runner minutes ran out: "recent account payments have failed"); public = free runners. Commit trailer: only `Co-Authored-By: dipa-studio <183933817+dipa-studio@users.noreply.github.com>`.

**Phone workflow (user does buttons, Claude runs commands):**
- Boot test kernel: phone in fastboot -> `fastboot boot shim/out/shim-boot-X.img`. NEVER `fastboot getvar`.
- **Read logs WITHOUT filming:** after a test the user holds Power+VolDown -> fastboot -> `fastboot boot recovery/recovery.img` (OrangeFox, temp) -> wait for `adb shell true` -> `tools/sweet-log-pull.sh /run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/logs/<name>.txt` (reads the 4 MiB console ring the kernel keeps in the last 1025 blocks of userdata; patch 0047). Then `adb reboot bootloader` brings the phone back to fastboot without the user.
- **Flash a root image:** `fastboot flash -S 512M userdata <img>.simg` (7 min, 25 chunks). After a big flash the Xiaomi ABL fastboot hangs: next `fastboot boot` stalls at "Sending" -> the USER must re-enter fastboot by hand (Power+VolDown). Do not retry commands against the hung ABL. A flash right after `adb reboot bootloader` once failed on chunk 1 (USB protocol error): just retry. User must NOT unplug during flash (once interrupted at 25/25 -> reflash).
- **Edit the phone root without reflashing (same-length only):** find the file's raw offset in the local image (it is one extent; search the image for its first 4 KiB), compare with `adb exec-out dd if=/dev/block/sda34 ...`, then `adb push` changed 4 KiB blocks and `dd of=/dev/block/sda34 seek=... conv=notrunc,fsync`, read back. Recovery kernel has no hfsplus.
- **Pull the whole phone root** (12 min): `adb exec-out "dd if=/dev/block/sda34 bs=1048576 count=16384" > img`; mount ro with udisksctl or `sudo mount -t hfsplus -o loop,ro`. To write into a pulled image: `sudo fsck.hfsplus -fy` first (volume is never cleanly unmounted -> Linux mounts ro even with -o force), then `sudo mount -t hfsplus -o loop,force`.
- Making images: copy latest img with `cp --sparse=always`, mount rw with force, change, `sync; umount`, `sha256sum`, `img2simg`. Keep only the latest image + one fallback on the SSD (/home is ~98% full; big work files live in `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/`, old kernels/images archived in `.../ios-sweet-work/archive/`).

**Diagnostics built into the kernel (all SWEET-only):** persistent console log (0047, boot-arg sweetlog=0 off; flush every ~1 s); `|name| text` copies of /dev/null writes for processes in `sweetnull` (default backboardd,SpringBoard; 0048); `IOK name:` IOKit lookup/open trace for `sweetiolog` (default backboardd,SpringBoard; 0049/0054, 400 lines max); `SWEET Sandbox: proc op N` (0050); `STR ...` heap text dump on SIGABRT/SIGTRAP for `sweetscan` (default backboardd,SpringBoard; 0051/0053) - this is how NSException/BSAssert reasons were found; `SMP name tN pc .. bt ..` user stack sampler for `sweetsample` (default SpringBoard,backboardd; first at 90 s then every 120 s, 40 samples, 16 frames; 0054/0055); `SWEET FB: N swaps` every 1000 swaps; `CRASH name ...` crash reports (0043/0044). Symbolicate cache addresses with `ipsw dyld a2s research/dsc/dyld_shared_cache_arm64 <addr - slide>`; main-binary addresses: addr - mh + 0x100000000.

**Patches added today (0046-0057, all CI-built):** 0046 IOSurface (IOCoreSurfaceRoot) + IOMobileFramebuffer (AppleCLCD on /arm-io/disp0) + arm-io nubs; 0047 persistent log; 0048 /dev/null tee; 0049 IOKit trace; 0050 permissive "Sandbox" MAC policy; 0051/0053 heap text on crash; 0052 RENAME_SWAP via exchangedata on HFS; 0054/0055 sampler, swap counter, AKS selectors 35 (extended device state = 17) and 101; 0056 panel copy from the vsync timer at 10 Hz (sweetfb_hz) instead of every swap; 0057 no config_ext_resolver (ACL group entries resolve without memberd). ADT (tools/mkadt.py): /arm-io/disp0, /chosen/display-scale 3, /chosen/unique-chip-id (synthetic 0x5357454554000001) + chip-id 0x8015 -> IOPlatformUUID A9683613-3704-53F2-95D0-98A8FB574E78. Image v9-v12: /private/var/containers/{Bundle,Data/System,Shared/SystemGroup,Temp}; launchd.plist StandardOut/ErrorPath renamed to ...PatX (pointing jobs at /dev/console stops them launching: EACCES); v12: LastSystemVersion=20H392 in plain /var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.apple.migration.plist (stops the 1-hour data migration that ran every boot), VirtualAudio.plugin moved to /Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL.disabled/.

- **v11 still migrated (boot 0056u, 20:32 UTC, `logs/boot-0056u-v11.txt`).** On iOS kCFPreferencesCurrentHost lands in the plain plist: all DMEnvironment setters (setLastBuildVersionPref, setUserDataDispositionPref, ...) use CurrentHost and the phone's `/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.apple.migration.plist` holds DMUserDataDisposition; datamigrator's XPC service runs with `_RoleAccount: mobile`. **Image v12** = v11 + `LastSystemVersion` = 20H392 added to that plain `com.apple.migration.plist` (keeps DMUserDataDisposition, 501:501 0600) + `/Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL/VirtualAudio.plugin` moved to `/Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL.disabled/` (Codex: stops the mediaserverd SIGSEGV loop; untested). **installcoordinationd loop explained (Codex `codex/installcoordinationd-report.md`):** os_crash "Failed to create data directory" EACCES for `/private/var/containers/Shared/SystemGroup/systemgroup.com.apple.installcoordinationd/Library/InstallCoordination`: `Library`/`Library/Caches` in the container are ACL-only (uid/gid 0xfffffffe, mode 0000, ACE for group GUID ...012B = gid 299 systemusers); with `config_ext_resolver` the kernel asks an identity resolver (memberd/opendirectoryd) that iOS does not run -> EWOULDBLOCK -> ACE skipped -> EACCES. **Patch 0057**: drop config_ext_resolver from FILESYS_BASE (compatibility UID/GID<->GUID mapping is used instead), likely fixes other ACL-protected containers too. Codex task 3: UFS driver performance design (`codex/ufs-performance.md`).
- **mediaserverd SIGSEGV loop explained (Codex report `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/codex/mediaserverd-report.md`):** the fault (FAR 0x390) is in the HAL plug-in `/Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL/VirtualAudio.plugin/VirtualAudio` (ApplyBeamFormerPreference <- VirtualAudio_PlugIn::SetPropertyData <- HALS_AHPPlugIn::ObjectSetPropertyData <- AudioObjectSetPropertyData <- MediaExperience vaemVADSerializationListener): the default virtual audio device ('vdef', VirtualAudioDevice_Default) does not exist (no audio hardware/driver, no /product/audio). Proposed workaround for GUI bring-up: move VirtualAudio.plugin out of /Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/HAL/ in the next root image (untested). v11 reflashed OK after an interrupted flash (USB unplugged at chunk 25/25). Codex task 2: installcoordinationd crash loop.
- **ROOT CAUSE of the endless migration: no IOPlatformUUID (2026-10-17).** datamigrator's `-[DMMigrator isMigrationNeededWithBuildVersion:lastBuildVersion:clientPID:]` gets lastBuildVersion from `-[DMEnvironment lastBuildVersionPref]` = `CFPreferencesCopyValue("LastSystemVersion", "com.apple.migration", kCFPreferencesCurrentUser, kCFPreferencesCurrentHost)` -> a ByHost file. The phone had `/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.apple.migration.plist` (only DMUserDataDisposition) and NO `Preferences/ByHost` directory at all. On arm XNU publishes IOPlatformUUID (gethostuuid) only from `/chosen/unique-chip-id` (8 bytes) + `/chosen/chip-id` (4 bytes) (IOPlatformExpertDevice::generatePlatformUUID, called from IOPlatformExpert::start); the SWEET ADT had neither, so every CurrentHost preference write failed. Fix: mkadt adds `unique-chip-id` = 0x5357454554000001 (synthetic) and `chip-id` = 0x8015 -> UUID `A9683613-3704-53F2-95D0-98A8FB574E78` (same SHA1 namespace computation as the kernel). ADT `shim/out/sweet-0057hi.adt`, boot image `shim/out/shim-boot-0056u.img` (0056 kernel + 0057hi ADT, QEMU OK). **Image v11** = v10 + `Preferences/ByHost/com.apple.migration.<UUID upper and lower>.plist` {LastSystemVersion: 20H392} (501:501, dir 0755 files 0600). Flashing v11 2026-10-17 ~20:10 UTC. Codex (gpt-6.1-sol high) analysing the mediaserverd SIGSEGV loop in `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/codex/` (report `mediaserverd-report.md`).
- **v10 did NOT stop the migration (boot 0056n on v10, 19:18 UTC, `logs/boot-0056n-v10.txt`):** datamigrator + migrationpluginwrapper ran again, backboardd drew the progress UI (13000+ swaps in ~5 min). So `com.apple.DataMigration.plist`/LastSystemVersion is not the deciding input. What is known about the decision (datamigrator XPC service `DataMigration.framework/XPCServices/com.apple.datamigrator.xpc/com.apple.datamigrator`, strings): `Migration sentinel present. Migration necessary.` (sentinel `/private/var/datamigrator/migrate`, created and removed by datamigrator itself; absent in v10), `System build version changed from %@ to %@. Migration necessary.` / `...unchanged... not necessary`, `Disposition dictionary with build version %@ is out of date relative to current build version %@`, `SyntheticDidUpgrade`, DMUserDataDispositionManager; previous build source not found yet (not in the binary's plain strings; likely in DataMigration.framework's DMUserDataDispositionManager / preferences domain `com.apple.migration`). backboardd calls `DMPerformMigrationIfNeeded` and holds SpringBoard in `checkInAndWaitForDataMigration` until it returns. NEXT: find where the previous build version / disposition dictionary is stored (disassemble DataMigration.framework DMUserDataDispositionManager + datamigrator's isMigrationNeeded path), write it into the image; or get datamigrator's os_log out (OS_ACTIVITY_DT_MODE needs a launchd env change).

Sampler (16 frames): **SpringBoard waits in `-[BKSSystemShellService _checkIn]`** (synchronous BSXPC call to backboardd, from FBSystemShellInitialize <- SBSystemAppMain) - backboardd holds the system shell check-in until the data migration ends, so the migration is the gate to SpringBoard UI. **Patch 0056**: swap only records the latest swap record; the vsync timer copies it to the panel every 60/sweetfb_hz ticks (default 10 Hz); `SWEET FB: N swaps` every 1000.

Sample during migration: SpringBoard main thread blocked in a synchronous XPC call (`mach_msg2_trap <- dispatch_mach_send_and_wait_for_reply <- xpc_connection_send_message_with_reply_sync <- -[BSXPCServiceConnectionMessage _sendSynchronously:] <- BSXPCServiceConnectionProxy ...`). Unsupported AKS selectors seen: 101 `aks_assert_consume`, 93 `aks_get_internal_info`, 34 `_aks_stash_load` (datamigrator), **35 `aks_get_extended_device_state`** (SpringBoard, chronod, remindd, searchd, symptomsd...). **Patch 0055**: AKS 35 answered like 17 (same DER device state), 101 succeeds; sampler backtraces 16 frames, 40 samples (~80 min).
- **MILESTONE: FIRST iOS PIXELS ON THE PANEL (boot 0053s long run, 15:33 UTC, ~60 min; photos IMG_20261017_183029/183222/183419; log `/run/media/dipa/android/ios-sweet-work/logs/boot-0053s-long.txt`, 407 KB, 1428 flushes).** The iOS data-migration boot screen (Apple logo + progress bar) was shown and the bar advanced to full in ~60 minutes. It is drawn by **backboardd through our kernel display path**: `SWEET SURF: backboardd create id 2/3 1080x2400 fmt 0x42475241 (BGRA) bpe 4 bpr 4352 size 10452992 keys 11`, IOSurface sels 9, 27, 5, 34 and traps 2/3 (lock/unlock), IOMFB SwapBegin (4) / SwapEnd (5, 1132-byte struct) / GetLayerDefaultSurface (3) / 68 / 55, then `SWEET FB: swap N layer 0 surface 2|3 1080x2400 ... a 0,0 1080x2400 b 0,0 1080x2400` alternating (double buffering; only the first 16 swaps are logged). Triggered by `com.apple.datamigrator` (AKS sels 101/93/34 return unsupported) and `com.apple.migrationpluginwrapper`. Probably ProgressUI-style CoreGraphics drawing into IOSurfaces (no GPU used). After the migration: SpringBoard and backboardd stay up (exec once each, no crash), nothing new drawn; crash loops only for mediaserverd, installcoordinationd, mobileactivationd (~108 each in the hour); bluetoothd and fairplayd.H2 relaunch constantly (no hardware). ADT `shim/out/sweet-0054hi.adt` (QEMU `research/q0054-hi.adt`), image `shim/out/shim-boot-0053s.img` = 0053 kernel + 0054hi ADT. Possible catch: MobileGestalt may have cached scale 0 in `/private/var/containers/Shared/SystemGroup/systemgroup.com.apple.mobilegestaltcache/...`.
- **MILESTONE: backboardd survives and opens the display (boot 0052n on v9, 15:07 UTC, `logs/boot-0052n.txt`).** No `CRASH backboardd`. It walks `IODeviceTree:/arm-io` children, matches IOMobileFramebuffer, `open AppleCLCD type 0 -> 0x0`, calls IOMFB selectors 57, 15, 22, 29, 33, 79 (GetBlock, out 1580/40), 74 (SupportedFrameInfo), 12, 6, 27, 17, 84, 50; opens IOCoreSurfaceRoot (sel 13); looks for AGXAccelerator / IOAcceleratorES / IOAcceleratorESLegacy (none, repeatedly), `IODeviceTree:/product` (none, repeatedly), `/buttons`, aop-smart-cover, IOPMrootDomain. No swaps yet. **SpringBoard now gets past display discovery** and dies (sig 5) in `-[FBSDisplayMode _initWithWidth:height:preferredScale:scaleOverride:refreshRate:gamut:hdr:rotation:virtual:validityCheck:]+580` <- `_initWithCADisplayMode:scale:rotation:` <- `FBSDisplayConfiguration _initWithImmutableDisplay:` <- `FBSDisplaySource initWithDisplay:` <- `FBSDisplayMonitor` (slide 0x14504000): "FBSDisplayMode cannot be configured as requested". Checks: width/height >= 2, preferredScale > 0, scaleOverride >= 0, refreshRate non-zero, valid gamut/HDR/rotation. Suspects: refresh rate 0 (IOMFB timing blocks answered with zeros) and/or scale 0 (MobileGestalt main-screen-scale; no /product node in the ADT). **Patch 0053**: heap text scan also on SIGTRAP and for SpringBoard (default sweetscan list backboardd,SpringBoard) to read the exact mode description.

Flashing v9 started 2026-10-17 ~15:00 UTC.
